2026-10-30T14:42:35.6175755Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the round-8 PP4 bundle (pinned selections, gigatoken tokenizer, gated diagonal-trim FA3 row) with a fresh image

One image build and one PP4 serving run at concurrency 232 on top of the
9832.74 configuration (41005872470). Round 7 (41040348302) adopted the
pipelined FA3 row (PP0 period 115.0/115.5/118.6 to 114.6/115.0/118.3 ms)
but hit the 17-minute limit because its serial in-job selection chain took
7.3 min. No profile pass is requested in this message.

What changed since round 7:
1. 3e49441 pins every settled selection (FA3 pipe row, MegaMoE
   four-stage-lean-split, onepass MoE input, fp32 Q/K norm, Decode fused
   MoE input and QKV 192x80, copy transport) and drops the in-job
   selection chain.
2. 249e24b removes the round-7 losers from the image: the nondeterministic
   MegaMoE shared reciprocal and the onepass-split and fp32-heads writers.
   This is why the image must be rebuilt.
3. 822fe41 serves the PP4 Prefill and Decode workers with the gigatoken
   tokenizer backend. All about 125 TTFT failures of the recent runs are in
   the profiling-start burst, where the single Decode TokenizerManager takes
   in 232 requests at about 7.4 ms each; gigatoken encodes the official
   prompts to identical token ids in about 2% of the HF time.
4. 534a68c and b2be6fb add the diagonal-trim Prefill FA3 row
   n192-direct-o-varlen-pipe-diag: on each tile's diagonal KV block, mask
   and softmax run only on the leading 32-key sub-blocks that some row of
   the warpgroup can see, and the P bytes of the rest are written as zero,
   the value the full block gives them. QK and PV are unchanged, so the
   output must be byte-equal to the pin. The pinned row's SASS is
   unchanged. Expected 9-12 us per sequence, 0.4-0.6 ms per period. It has
   never served, so the job screens it against the pin on all twelve
   Prefill GPUs and runs its 1200-call endurance gate; any loss, failure
   or hang keeps the pin. This adds about 1.2-1.5 min before worker start.

Expected: about +0.3% from the burst and up to +0.5% from the FA3 trim on
top of the round-7 period, roughly 9,890-9,950. Job time estimated at
about 11.5 min of 17.

Validation: full pytest suite, bash -n on the sbatch; the FA3 build was
compiled for sm_90a with CUDA 13.4 (168 registers, spills equal to the
pin, no wgmma serialization warning).
